HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_22e89a171c3316b2004d","text":"The later fire section surprised me by making `غ ش و` more than darkness: 92:14-17 creates a cover/contact/avoidance geometry. Night’s covering now anticipates both dangerous engulfing and the need to be moved aside from the blaze.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000558/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3da1689da7e9922ffaee","text":"2. **The mantle that clings but does not catch.** The form `تَرَدَّىٰ` also activates `ر د ي:B004` (putting on a mantle and what clings to the shoulders), a branch whose scope includes debt as something attached to the neck. Possessive `مَالُهُ`, followed within the window by warning, fire, denial, and turning away in 92:14–16, makes the clothing image materially coherent: what is possessed can remain wrapped around a person while offering no arresting support. This is an exploratory double image; falling is the primary construction, and the mantle reading does not replace it. It changes the scene from simple dispossession to a sharper irony—wealth may cling through the fall yet still fail to bear its owner.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000558/B004","root_001457/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4909d8a430fc106c09f1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:11:4\",\"root_001457\",[\"B001\"]],[\"92:11:6\",\"root_000558\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Malın omuzdaki bir giysi gibi kişiye yapışık kalırken onu düşüşten tutamaması, ironik bir çift görüntü kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000558/B003","root_001110/B002","root_001457/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_639ca14ed03e15da2f9f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:11:2\",\"root_001110\",[\"B002\"]],[\"92:11:4\",\"root_001457\",[\"B001\"]],[\"92:11:6\",\"root_000558\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Servet, düşüş anında sahibinin yerine geçmesi veya onu tutması beklenen bir destek gibi sınanır ve tam o anda bu işlevi yerine getiremez.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6ec856cb4e6ced81c10f","text":"1. **Possession cannot substitute for the possessor.** `يُغْنِى` anchors `غ ن ي:B002` (sufficing, availing, and standing in), `مَالُهُ` anchors `م و ل:B001` (acquired wealth), and `تَرَدَّىٰ` anchors `ر د ي:B003` (falling into ruin). The sequence explicitly places wealth in the attempted functional role of arresting or replacing its owner at the moment of descent, then negates that role. The minimal inference is that “availing” is being tested as support, while the packet directly supplies the negation and fall. The ayah reads as a failed substitution mechanism, not merely a statement that wealth is generally useless.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000558/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b1e7025522e901676076","text":"2. **Falling as mantle reversed.** Evidence: `ر د ي` B004 (mantle/clinging to shoulders), activated by 92:1 covering and 92:17 being kept aside from fire. Structural cue: the verb can evoke both falling and being wrapped. Mechanism: what clung as property/cover becomes unable to clothe or shield. Abductive move: I infer a reversal from protective covering to ruinous draping. Reading change: “تردى” can be read as collapse into the very covering one trusted.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000558/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c2cc75db3ee59554cc17","text":"92:14–16 turned the fall from an isolated accident into the terminus of warning, encounter, denial, and withdrawal. Those later ayat also made the mantle branch `ر د ي:B004` surprisingly apt: possession may remain attached while the person loses direction and support, though the falling branch `B003` remains primary.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000558/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c56b9384379414f53505","text":"3. **Stone/weight imagery of descent.** Evidence: `ر د ي` B001 (stone throwing/rock), B002 (heavy trampling/bounding gait), activated by `ع ل و` highness in 92:20. Structural cue: the sequence moves from wealth to falling, then later to the Highest. Mechanism: amassed property behaves like downward mass rather than upward rank. Abductive move: I infer gravitational moral physics: misplaced weight pulls down. Reading change: wealth is not neutral cargo; it can become the stone-weight of descent.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000558/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d04d40e3728b837d9af5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:11:6\",\"root_000558\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Daha sonraki uyarı, ateş, inkâr ve yüz çevirme, düşüşü tekil bir kazadan çıkarıp uyarılmış bir yolun varış noktasına dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000558/B001","root_000558/B002","root_001457/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_daa37fc4bd5b85c15327","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:11:4\",\"root_001457\",[\"B001\"]],[\"92:11:6\",\"root_000558\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Taş ve ağır adım boyutları, biriktirilen malı yükselten bir değer yerine kişiyi aşağı çeken ağırlık gibi düşündürür; bu fiziksel benzetme zayıftır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ec80720176a42b52126c","text":"1. **Wealth failing as substitute.** Evidence: `غ ن ي` B002 (sufficing/availing/replacing), `م و ل` B001 (wealth/property), `ر د ي` B003 (falling into ruin). Structural cue: 92:8 claimed self-sufficiency; this ayah negates its instrument. Mechanism: money is tested as a stand-in and cannot accompany the falling self. Abductive move: I infer a moment of need in which stored wealth is asked to function as rescue. Reading change: the ayah is not general poverty-of-wealth; it is failed substitution at the instant of collapse.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
